# Skills · T · part 71

780 entries, tft-clock to thalarch-spec. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **tft-clock**: Design and animate clock faces, animations and pixel-art scenes for small colour TFT/LCD modules, starting with the Luat Air101-LCD (0.96" IPS, 160x80, ST7735S, SPI). Use when asked to design, redesign or animate a clock face or scene for an ST7735 / 160x80 colour LCD, to make one look unlike the usual library-font clock, or to check that a face will run on the hardware (SPI time, refresh and tearing, per-frame workload on a small MCU). Provides an ST7735S emulator that runs real command bytes, an RGB565 canvas with hard and anti-aliased drawing and dithered gradients, a renderer of the glass… ([MayHappybb/clock-skills](https://github.com/MayHappybb/clock-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/tft-clock))
- **tft-coach**: Coach Rai at Teamfight Tactics (TFT) mid-game with current, factual data. Use for ANY TFT question (augments, items, units, traits, comps, rolling odds, economy, what to buy or pivot into). Entry skill that routes to tft-data, tft-explorer, tft-meta, tft-set. ([rbutera/dotfiles](https://github.com/rbutera/dotfiles/tree/HEAD/dot_claude/skills/tft-coach) · ★1)
- **tft-data**: Look up TFT facts on datatft.com - the /rate cheat sheet (shop odds by level, XP, gold interest, streak gold, augment and orb loot tables, one anchor per loot augment), unit/item/trait/emblem pages, tier lists, comps, explorer. Use when Rai names an augment/item/unit/trait or asks for odds, interest, economy or loot. Includes the tft-url CLI. ([rbutera/dotfiles](https://github.com/rbutera/dotfiles/tree/HEAD/dot_claude/skills/tft-data) · ★1)
- **tft-explorer**: Explorer stats for SPECIFIC TFT combinations - "unit X holding items A+B, what is the best third item", "emblem A + emblem B placements", "unit + trait tier performance". Uses MetaTFT's explorer API through the tft-explore CLI (no browser). Use instead of generic tier lists whenever Rai names a combination. ([rbutera/dotfiles](https://github.com/rbutera/dotfiles/tree/HEAD/dot_claude/skills/tft-explorer) · ★1)
- **tft-guidelines**: AskUserQuestion 사용 시점과 방식 판단. 언제 묻고 언제 안 묻을지 결정하는 철학. 세션 시작 시 필수 로드. ([heestolee/pilee](https://github.com/heestolee/pilee/tree/HEAD/skills/tft-guidelines) · ★3)
- **tft-meta**: Current TFT meta from tftflow.com - which comps are Default/Meta/Other, their economy plan, and the CONDITIONS (items, artifacts, emblems, augments) that raise a comp's tier. Use for "what's meta", "is comp X good", "what makes X work", "should I go X with what I have". ([rbutera/dotfiles](https://github.com/rbutera/dotfiles/tree/HEAD/dot_claude/skills/tft-meta) · ★1)
- **tft-set**: Reference for the CURRENT TFT set and patch - units by cost with traits, trait breakpoints, and the patch's character (fast 8/9 vs reroll mix, nerfs and buffs). Data lives in set.md and set.json next to this file with the patch and refresh date. Use for questions about traits, synergies, what a unit is, what the patch favours. ([rbutera/dotfiles](https://github.com/rbutera/dotfiles/tree/HEAD/dot_claude/skills/tft-set) · ★1)
- **tftcoach**: Use this skill when the user asks about Teamfight Tactics (TFT) — comps, items, augments, economy, pivoting, positioning, or any gameplay advice. Triggers: 'tftcoach', 'tft', 'teamfight tactics', 'what comp should I play', 'what items should I build', 'should I pivot', 'which augment', 'tft help', 'when to level', 'how to play tft'. Reads board state from screenshots or descriptions, gives real-time coaching on comps, items, economy, augments, and pivoting. Do NOT use for: League of Legends (Summoner's Rift), other auto-battlers, or TFT account issues. ([KeWang0622/tftcoach](https://github.com/KeWang0622/tftcoach/blob/HEAD/SKILL.md) · ★6)
- **tftd-writing**: Too Fast to Die の全日本語テキスト（シーン・イベント・会話・台詞・カード説明・UIテキスト等）の執筆・推敲時に使用する汎用文章スキル。LLM的表現の禁止、冗長排除、推量と断定の区別、濃淡コントロール、PC別態度テンプレート、勢力別性文化、ネオン×性行為、妊娠エンド分岐を含む。「シーン」「イベント」「テキスト作成」「台詞」「会話」「カード説明」「説明文」「シナリオ」「物語」「ストーリー」「セックスシーン」「享楽イベント」「娼館シーン」「性的イベント」「ブルードマザーシーン」「同行者との宿泊」「繁殖政策イベント」「目撃シーン」の執筆依頼で発動する。 ([sashimihoutyou/too-fast-to-die](https://github.com/sashimihoutyou/too-fast-to-die/tree/HEAD/.agents/skills/tftd-writing) · 2 copies)

- **tfui-architecture**: tfui's core SDK abstractions: PluginDeps and the immutable Context, pins vs targets, event bus, plugin interface and optional interfaces, routing and navigation, action mode, Service and ServiceCache, source layer, macro engine, test harness, overlays and frames. - **tfui-tui-ux**: tfui TUI UX rules: navigation layers, NavReplace vs NavPush, frame stack, keybinding layers, cursor vs batch action model, actions bar vs hint bar, scroll indicators, inspect view, confirmation ownership, staleness guard, anti-patterns. Use before editing plugins/, internal/ui/ or pkg/sdk/frames/, or any View(), Hints() or key handling. ([lmarqs/terraform-ui](https://github.com/lmarqs/terraform-ui/tree/HEAD/.agents/skills/tfui-tui-ux) · ★6)

([genusarvicolabathos238/triflux](https://github.com/genusarvicolabathos238/triflux/tree/HEAD/skills/tfx-autoresearch) · ★1)
- **tfx-autoroute**: 작업 유형에 따라 최적 모델을 자동 선택하여 실행해야 할 때 사용한다. 'sisyphus', '시지프스', 'auto-route', '알아서 라우팅', '최적 모델로' 같은 요청에 사용. 어떤 CLI를 쓸지 모르겠을 때, 또는 실패 시 자동 모델 승격이 필요할 때 적극 활용. ([genusarvicolabathos238/triflux](https://github.com/genusarvicolabathos238/triflux/tree/HEAD/skills/tfx-autoroute) · ★1)

- **tfx-codex**: Codex-Only 오케스트레이터. tfx-auto 워크플로우를 Codex 전용으로 고정합니다. '코덱스로 해줘', '코덱스한테 시켜', 'codex로', 'Codex 전용' 같은 요청에 반드시 사용. ([genusarvicolabathos238/triflux](https://github.com/genusarvicolabathos238/triflux/tree/HEAD/skills/tfx-codex) · ★1)

'codex swarm', 'codex 다중', '코덱스 스웜', '코덱스 다중 실행', 'PRD 일괄 실행', 'codex spawn', '코덱스 스폰', '다중 코덱스', 'swarm codex', 'omx swarm', '새 창에서 진행', '새 창에서 prd', 'prd 부터', 'prd 실행', 'prd 돌려', '병렬로 돌려', 'worktree로 실행' 같은 요청에 반드시 사용. - **tfx-consensus**: 3자 합의 엔진 — 모든 Deep 스킬의 핵심 인프라. Claude/Codex/Gemini 독립 분석 결과를 교차검증하여 편향 없는 합의를 도출한다. 독립 실행도 가능: '합의로 분석해', '3자 합의', 'consensus' 같은 요청 시 직접 호출 가능. ([genusarvicolabathos238/triflux](https://github.com/genusarvicolabathos238/triflux/tree/HEAD/skills/tfx-consensus) · ★1)

Re-authorize an EXISTING Telegram agent: obtains a fresh session string via auth tools and overwrites only the sessionString field in the agent's tg-account.json. Use when an agent's session is dead — e.g. AUTH_KEY_DUPLICATED, account frozen, manual logout, or session revoked — and the agent already exists (do NOT recreate it). For creating a brand-new agent use tg-agent-create instead. ([Timur-Khabibulin/telegram-user-tool](https://github.com/Timur-Khabibulin/telegram-user-tool/tree/HEAD/skills/tg-agent-reauth))
- **tg-agent-reauth-from-tdata**: Re-authorizes an EXISTING Telegram agent using its own tdata session (Telegram Desktop), fully automatically — no manual code entry, no changes to profile/photos/channels/2FA. Use when an agent's session is dead (AUTH_KEY_DUPLICATED, need_update_session) AND a tdata folder for that account is available. Different from tg-agent-reauth (requires the operator to relay a code manually) and from tg-agent-autoregistration (creates a brand-new agent, destructive setup). Triggers: 'реавторизуй агента через tdata', 'tg-agent-reauth-from-tdata'. ([Timur-Khabibulin/telegram-user-tool](https://github.com/Timur-Khabibulin/telegram-user-tool/tree/HEAD/skills/tg-agent-reauth-from-tdata))

Forces explicit problem framing, competing hypotheses/approaches, disconfirmation, independent challenge, evidence-based adjudication, uncertainty tracking, and a final falsifiable proof without exposing private chain-of-thought or adding ceremony to trivial work. ([LUC4N3X/antigravity-thalarch](https://github.com/LUC4N3X/antigravity-thalarch/tree/HEAD/thalarch-mode/skills/thalarch-reasoning) · ★6)
- **thalarch-refactor**: Behavior-preserving refactoring protocol for simplifying, restructuring, modularizing, or modernizing existing code without silently changing externally observable behavior. Use for non-trivial cleanup, decomposition, abstraction changes, package/module moves, or legacy modernization where regression risk matters. ([LUC4N3X/antigravity-thalarch](https://github.com/LUC4N3X/antigravity-thalarch/tree/HEAD/thalarch-mode/skills/thalarch-refactor) · ★6)
- **thalarch-review**: Runs risk-sized, evidence-first review of code changes. Use before completion, PR creation, or after meaningful implementation. Separates requirement compliance from engineering quality, uses independent reviewer contexts and perspective shifts to break self-review blind spots, confirms findings before fixing them, and supports lite, standard, and deep review depth. ([LUC4N3X/antigravity-thalarch](https://github.com/LUC4N3X/antigravity-thalarch/tree/HEAD/thalarch-mode/skills/thalarch-review) · ★6)
- **thalarch-router**: Chooses the smallest compatible process, language, domain, platform, visual, cognitive, and installed-skill stack for a task. Use before complex work and after project discovery. Combines autonomous skill intelligence with task, stack, risk, evidence, source-grounding, context, no-regression, memory/experience, teacher/eval, and in-flight doubt routing instead of requiring the user to manually name the best skills. ([LUC4N3X/antigravity-thalarch](https://github.com/LUC4N3X/antigravity-thalarch/tree/HEAD/thalarch-mode/skills/thalarch-router) · ★6)
- **thalarch-rust**: Project-aware Rust engineering for libraries, services, CLIs, async applications, and systems code. Use for Rust source, Cargo workspaces/features, ownership/lifetimes, unsafe code, concurrency, tests, performance, or Rust-specific refactoring. ([LUC4N3X/antigravity-thalarch](https://github.com/LUC4N3X/antigravity-thalarch/tree/HEAD/thalarch-mode/skills/thalarch-rust) · ★6)
- **thalarch-security**: Performs evidence-backed application and agentic-workflow security review. Use for auth, authorization, untrusted input, secrets, cryptography, network exposure, command execution, file/path handling, dependencies, GitHub Actions, MCP/tool integrations, or any explicit security audit. Trace sources to sinks and confirm findings to reduce false positives. ([LUC4N3X/antigravity-thalarch](https://github.com/LUC4N3X/antigravity-thalarch/tree/HEAD/thalarch-mode/skills/thalarch-security) · ★6)
- **thalarch-skill-intelligence**: Autonomous skill-selection layer for Thalarch. Use at the start of non-trivial work and again after project discovery when the current host may expose stronger project-local, official platform, Thalarch, or third-party expertise. Shortlists the smallest high-value skill stack based on task fit, project/toolchain compatibility, authority/currentness, tool leverage, evidence needs, redundancy, conflicts, context cost, and actual host availability. ([LUC4N3X/antigravity-thalarch](https://github.com/LUC4N3X/antigravity-thalarch/tree/HEAD/thalarch-mode/skills/thalarch-skill-intelligence) · ★6)
- **thalarch-source-grounding**: Grounds version-sensitive framework, library, runtime, browser, database, and platform decisions in the exact project version plus current primary documentation. Use before implementing or reviewing non-trivial external APIs, configuration, migration guidance, compatibility behavior, or framework-specific patterns where model memory can be stale. ([LUC4N3X/antigravity-thalarch](https://github.com/LUC4N3X/antigravity-thalarch/tree/HEAD/thalarch-mode/skills/thalarch-source-grounding) · ★6)
- **thalarch-spec**: Turns broad feature or architecture requests into an executable acceptance contract. Use for multi-file features, refactors, migrations, architecture work, ambiguous behavior, or whenever implementation could succeed technically while missing the user's actual intent.
